<a id="pm-s1"></a>
## 1. Abstract and result contract

> We solve the free group factor isomorphism problem affirmatively by proving that $`L(\mathbb F_2)`$ and $`L(\mathbb F_3)`$ are isomorphic as tracial von Neumann algebras. The classical free group factor alternative then implies that all interpolated free group factors, including $`L(\mathbb F_\infty)`$, are isomorphic and have fundamental group $`\mathbb R_{\gt 0}`$.

- **C1** (theorem, [source](#stmt-thm-rank-step)): For every integer $n\geq3$ there is a unital trace-preserving normal $*$-isomorphism $$L(\mathbb F_n)\cong L(\mathbb F_{n+1}).$$
  - Explanation: States a normal tracial isomorphism between consecutive free group ranks in the stated integer regime.
- **C2** (theorem, [source](#stmt-thm-main)): There exists a unital normal trace-preserving $*$-isomorphism $$\Phi:L(\mathbb F_2)\longrightarrow L(\mathbb F_3).$$
  - Explanation: States the normal tracial isomorphism between the rank-two and rank-three free group factors.
- **C3** (theorem, [source](#stmt-lem-haar-identification)): For a freely generating Haar $d$-tuple $U$ in $M$, the assignment $\lambda(x_j)\mapsto U_j$ extends to a unital normal trace-preserving $*$-isomorphism $L(\mathbb F_d)\to M$.
  - Explanation: Identifies the algebra through a Haar tuple that also generates the whole ambient algebra.
- **C4** (theorem, [source](#stmt-lem-conjugates)): The algebras $A_gW^*(C)A_g^*$, $g\in\Gamma$, form a free family. In particular, each $S_g$ is free from the von Neumann algebra generated by all $S_b$ with $b\ne g$.
  - Explanation: Establishes freeness of the conjugate component algebras used in the flow.
- **C5** (theorem, [source](#stmt-lem-labels)): A monomial with nonidentity label has trace zero. A monomial with identity label can be expressed as a product of variables $S_g$ (or as the constant $1$).
  - Explanation: Separates vanishing traces of nonidentity labels from the conjugate-logarithm representation of identity labels.
- **C6** (bound, [source](#stmt-lem-free-sum)): For every such $k$, $$\label{eq:free-sum} \left\lVert s(k)\right\rVert\leq K\left\lVert k\right\rVert_{\ell^2},\qquad K=3\pi.$$
  - Explanation: Bounds the operator norm by the coefficient norm independently of the finite support size.
- **C7** (construction, [source](#stmt-prop-flow)): There is a one-parameter group $(\beta_t)_{t\in\mathbb R}$ of unital trace-preserving normal $*$-automorphisms of $M$ that fixes $S$ and whose restriction to $$B=C^*(A_1,\ldots,A_n,S)$$ is point-norm continuous. Writing $A_j(t)=\beta_t(A_j)$ and evaluating group words at this tuple, the paths satisfy $$\frac{d}{dt}A_j(t)=\mathrm is_t(h_j)A_j(t),\qquad s_t(k)=\sum_{g\in\Gamma}k(g)A_g(t)SA_g(t)^*,\qquad A_j(0)=A_j. \label{eq:flow-ode}$$ The derivatives here are in operator norm. With $K=3\pi$ as in <a href="#eq:free-sum" data-reference-type="eqref" data-reference="eq:free-sum">[eq:free-sum]</a>, for every $t\in\mathbb R$, $$\begin{aligned} \left\lVert \beta_t(A_j)-A_j\right\rVert &\leq \|t\|K\\|h_j\\|_{\ell^2}, \label{eq:generator-displacement}\\ \left\lVert \beta_t(A_w)-\exp(\mathrm itS)A_w\right\rVert &\leq \|t\|K\\|D_w-\delta_e\\|_{\ell^2} \qquad(w\in\Gamma). \label{eq:word-displacement} \end{aligned}$$ In particular, at $t=1$ the target in <a href="#eq:word-displacement" data-reference-type="eqref" data-reference="eq:word-displacement">[eq:word-displacement]</a> is $CA_w$.
  - Explanation: Combines a trace-preserving automorphism flow with generator and word displacement estimates.
- **C8** (theorem, [source](#stmt-lem-flow-trace-identity)): For every $p\in\mathcal P$ and every integer $r\geq1$, $$\tau\bigl(\operatorname{ev}_0(\delta^r p)\bigr)=0. \label{eq:universal-trace-identity}$$
  - Explanation: Establishes the initial trace identity for every positive iterated formal derivative.
- **C9** (theorem, [source](#stmt-lem-flow-global-ode)): Let $E$ be any closed unital $C^*$-subalgebra of $M$ containing $S$, and let $V_1,\ldots,V_n$ be unitaries in $E$. The equation $X'=\mathcal V(X)$ with $X(0)=V$ has a unique global solution in $E^n$ that is smooth in norm, and all its coordinates remain unitary.
  - Explanation: Provides global norm-smooth unitary solutions inside a closed generated algebra.
- **C10** (theorem, [source](#stmt-lem-flow-moments)): For every $p\in\mathcal P$ and $t\in\mathbb R$, $$\tau\bigl(\operatorname{ev}_t(p)\bigr) =\tau\bigl(\operatorname{ev}_0(p)\bigr). \label{eq:flow-moments}$$
  - Explanation: Preserves every formal polynomial moment along the actual solution.
- **C11** (construction, [source](#stmt-lem-small-cocycle)): Let $n\geq 3$ and $\eta>0$. There exist a word $w\in\Gamma$ and a finitely supported real coefficient vector $h$ such that the cocycle determined by $$h_1=h,\qquad h_j=0\quad(2\leq j\leq n)$$ satisfies $$\label{eq:small-cocycle} \\|h\\|_{\ell^2}<\eta, \qquad \\|D_w-\delta_e\\|_{\ell^2}<\eta.$$
  - Explanation: Obtains a finitely supported real coefficient vector with small norm and a prescribed approximate word value.
- **C12** (theorem, [source](#stmt-lem-cocycle-prefixes)): For each $m\geq 1$, the elements $p_1,\ldots,p_m$ freely generate a free subgroup of $\Gamma$. If $h_1=h$ and $h_j=0$ for $j\geq 2$, then $$\label{eq:cocycle-word-value} D_{w_m}=T_m h.$$
  - Explanation: Establishes a free prefix family and the exact cocycle evaluation formula.
- **C13** (theorem, [source](#stmt-lem-cocycle-spectrum)): The measures $\mu_m$ converge weakly to the probability measure $$\label{eq:cocycle-limit-density} d\nu(x)=\frac{1}{2\pi}\sqrt{\frac{4-x}{x}} \,1_{(0,4)}(x)\,dx.$$ In particular, $\nu(\{0\})=0$.
  - Explanation: Identifies the weak spectral limit and its absence of an atom at the origin.
- **C14** (construction, [source](#stmt-prop-perturbation)): Let $n\geq 3$, and let $(A_1,\ldots,A_n,C)$ be a freely generating Haar tuple in a finite von Neumann algebra $(M,\tau)$. For every $\varepsilon>0$ there are a trace-preserving normal automorphism $\alpha$ of $M$ and a word $w\in\mathbb F_n$ such that, with $A_j'=\alpha(A_j)$, $$\label{eq:perturbation} \max_{1\leq j\leq n}\left\lVert A_j'-A_j\right\rVert<\varepsilon, \qquad \left\lVert A_w'-C\right\rVert<\varepsilon.$$ The tuple $(A_1',\ldots,A_n',C')$, where $C'=\alpha(C)$, is again freely generating Haar. Here the target in the second estimate is the original $C$; the new completion is $$C'=C A_w^*,$$ where $A_w$ is evaluated on the original $n$-tuple.
  - Explanation: Approximates the original completion by a word in the moved tuple while retaining a freely generating completed tuple.
- **C15** (theorem, [source](#stmt-cor-all-interpolated)): For all $1<r,s\leq\infty$ there is a unital normal trace-preserving $*$-isomorphism $L(\mathbb F_r)\cong L(\mathbb F_s)$. Moreover, $$\mathcal F\bigl(L(\mathbb F_r)\bigr)=\mathbb R_{>0} \qquad(1<r\leq\infty).$$
  - Explanation: States the interpolated isomorphisms and the full positive-real fundamental group as consequences.
- **C16** (theorem, [source](#stmt-cor-entropy-dimensions)): Let $M=L(\mathbb F_2)$ with its canonical trace. For every integer $n\geq2$ there are a self-adjoint $n$-tuple $X^{(n)}$ and a self-adjoint $2n$-tuple $Y^{(n)}$ such that $$W^*(X^{(n)})=W^*(Y^{(n)})=M$$ and $$\delta(X^{(n)})=\delta_0(X^{(n)})=n, \qquad \delta^*(Y^{(n)})=\delta^\star(Y^{(n)})=n.$$ Consequently, none of $\delta,\delta_0,\delta^*,\delta^\star$ is invariant under changing a finite self-adjoint $W^*$-generating tuple of a tracial von Neumann algebra. Each takes unbounded values on such generating tuples of the single factor $L(\mathbb F_2)$.
  - Explanation: Distinguishes invariance of joint distributions from invariance under replacing von Neumann generators.

        "anchor": "stmt-prop-flow",
        "exact_quote": "There is a one-parameter group $(\\beta_t)_{t\\in\\mathbb R}$ of unital trace-preserving normal $*$-automorphisms of $M$ that fixes $S$ and whose restriction to $$B=C^*(A_1,\\ldots,A_n,S)$$ is point-norm continuous. Writing $A_j(t)=\\beta_t(A_j)$ and evaluating group words at this tuple, the paths satisfy $$\\frac{d}{dt}A_j(t)=\\mathrm is_t(h_j)A_j(t),\\qquad\n s_t(k)=\\sum_{g\\in\\Gamma}k(g)A_g(t)SA_g(t)^*,\\qquad A_j(0)=A_j.\n \\label{eq:flow-ode}$$ The derivatives here are in operator norm. With $K=3\\pi$ as in <a href=\"#eq:free-sum\" data-reference-type=\"eqref\" data-reference=\"eq:free-sum\">[eq:free-sum]</a>, for every $t\\in\\mathbb R$, $$\\begin{aligned}\n \\left\\lVert \\beta_t(A_j)-A_j\\right\\rVert\n &\\leq |t|K\\|h_j\\|_{\\ell^2},\n \\label{eq:generator-displacement}\\\\\n \\left\\lVert \\beta_t(A_w)-\\exp(\\mathrm itS)A_w\\right\\rVert\n &\\leq |t|K\\|D_w-\\delta_e\\|_{\\ell^2}\n \\qquad(w\\in\\Gamma).\n \\label{eq:word-displacement}\n\\end{aligned}$$ In particular, at $t=1$ the target in <a href=\"#eq:word-displacement\" data-reference-type=\"eqref\" data-reference=\"eq:word-displacement\">[eq:word-displacement]</a> is $CA_w$.",
        "source_id": "canonical:PM-287.1"
      },
      "evidence": [
        {
          "anchor": "stmt-prop-flow",
          "exact_quote": "There is a one-parameter group $(\\beta_t)_{t\\in\\mathbb R}$ of unital trace-preserving normal $*$-automorphisms of $M$ that fixes $S$ and whose restriction to $$B=C^*(A_1,\\ldots,A_n,S)$$ is point-norm continuous.       "conclusion": {
        "anchor": "stmt-prop-perturbation",
        "exact_quote": "Let $n\\geq 3$, and let $(A_1,\\ldots,A_n,C)$ be a freely generating Haar tuple in a finite von Neumann algebra $(M,\\tau)$. For every $\\varepsilon>0$ there are a trace-preserving normal automorphism $\\alpha$ of $M$ and a word $w\\in\\mathbb F_n$ such that, with $A_j'=\\alpha(A_j)$, $$\\label{eq:perturbation}\n \\max_{1\\leq j\\leq n}\\left\\lVert A_j'-A_j\\right\\rVert<\\varepsilon,\n \\qquad\n \\left\\lVert A_w'-C\\right\\rVert<\\varepsilon.$$ The tuple $(A_1',\\ldots,A_n',C')$, where $C'=\\alpha(C)$, is again freely generating Haar.         "source_id": "canonical:PM-287.1"
      },

        "exact_quote": "Let $M=L(\\mathbb F_2)$ with its canonical trace. <a id="pm-s2"></a>
## 2. Definitions, validity and bounds

- **Group von Neumann algebra** ([source](#pm-h-2)): For a discrete group $G$, its group von Neumann algebra is $$L(G)=\{\lambda(g):g\in G\}'',\qquad (\lambda(g)\xi)(h)=\xi(g^{-1}h),\quad \xi\in\ell^2(G),$$ with canonical trace $\tau(x)=\langle x\delta_e,\delta_e\rangle$.
- **Free groups** ([source](#pm-h-2)): Write $\mathbb F_n$ for the free group on $n$ generators and $\mathbb F_\infty$ for the free group on countably infinitely many generators.
- **Freely generating Haar tuple** ([source](#stmt-auto-definition-1)): A *freely generating Haar $d$-tuple* in $M$ is a tuple of unitaries $U=(U_1,\ldots,U_d)$ such that $W^*(U)=M$ and $$\tau(U_g)=0\qquad(g\in\mathbb F_d\setminus\{e\}).$$ Here $U_g$ is the evaluation of the reduced group word $g$ in $U$, with inverse letters evaluated as adjoints.
- **Freeness** ([source](#sec:prelim)): A family $(B_i)_{i\in I}$ of unital subalgebras is *free* if, for every integer $r\geq1$ and every choice of elements satisfying the conditions below, $$\tau(b_1\cdots b_r)=0 \quad\text{whenever}\quad b_j\in B_{i_j},\quad \tau(b_j)=0,\quad i_j\ne i_{j+1}\ (1\leq j<r).$$
- **Coefficient free sum** ([source](#pm-h-6)): For a finitely supported real function $k$ on $\Gamma$, set $$s(k)=\sum_{g\in\Gamma}k(g)S_g.$$
- **Completion** ([source](#sec:generation)): We call $C$ a *completion* of $A=(A_1,\ldots,A_n)$ when $(A,C)$ is a freely generating Haar tuple in $M$.
- **Amplification convention** ([source](#sec:amplification)): We recall the amplification convention for a $\mathrm{II}_1$ factor $(Q,\tau_Q)$. Let $\mathop{\mathrm{Tr}}$ be the usual semifinite trace on $B(\ell^2)$, normalized to give every rank-one projection trace one. For $t>0$, choose a projection $p$ in the stabilized factor with $(\tau_Q\otimes\mathop{\mathrm{Tr}})(p)=t$ and set $$Q^t=p\bigl(Q\,\overline\otimes\,B(\ell^2)\bigr)p, \qquad \tau_{Q^t}=t^{-1}(\tau_Q\otimes\mathop{\mathrm{Tr}})\big\|_{Q^t}.$$ Projections of every positive finite trace exist in this stabilized factor. Equivalence of projections of equal finite trace makes the normalized corner independent of the choice of $p$, up to a normal trace-preserving $*$-isomorphism.

<a id="pm-s3"></a>
## 3. Proof roadmap

- **lem-haar-identification** [claim](#stmt-lem-haar-identification) / [proof](#proof-stmt-lem-haar-identification): Spatial identification using the orthonormal word vectors and normal left representations. -> A normal tracial isomorphism.
- **lem-conjugates** [claim](#stmt-lem-conjugates) / [proof](#proof-stmt-lem-conjugates): Cyclic trace rewriting and freeness of the original components. -> A free conjugate family.
- **lem-labels** [claim](#stmt-lem-labels) / [proof](#proof-stmt-lem-labels): Centering, free reduction, and moving group factors to the right. -> Trace vanishing and conjugate-product representation.
- **lem-free-sum** [claim](#stmt-lem-free-sum) / [proof](#proof-stmt-lem-free-sum): Creation, annihilation, and diagonal blocks in the reduced tensor representation. -> A support-independent norm bound.
- **lem-flow-trace-identity** [claim](#stmt-lem-flow-trace-identity) / [proof](#proof-stmt-lem-flow-trace-identity): Differentiate law-preserving conjugations at the initial tuple and apply the formal derivation repeatedly. -> Vanishing initial traces of iterated derivatives.
- **lem-flow-global-ode** [claim](#stmt-lem-flow-global-ode) / [proof](#proof-stmt-lem-flow-global-ode): Local polynomial existence, conservation of unitarity, and continuation from bounded derivatives. -> Global norm-smooth unitary solution.
- **lem-flow-moments** [claim](#stmt-lem-flow-moments) / [proof](#proof-stmt-lem-flow-moments): Bound iterated derivatives and continue constant real-analytic moments along the real line. -> Actual moment invariance.
- **prop-flow** [claim](#stmt-prop-flow) / [proof](#proof-stmt-lem-flow-moments-2): Descend through relations, recover the original generators by reverse time, extend normally, and integrate displacement estimates. -> A normal tracial automorphism group with displacement bounds.
- **lem-cocycle-prefixes** [claim](#stmt-lem-cocycle-prefixes) / [proof](#proof-stmt-lem-cocycle-prefixes): Reduced free products and inverse basis transformations; sum coefficients translated by preceding prefixes. -> Free prefixes and the exact word cocycle value.
- **lem-cocycle-spectrum** [claim](#stmt-lem-cocycle-spectrum) / [proof](#proof-stmt-lem-cocycle-spectrum): Count noncrossing cancellations, pass moments through tight subsequences, and identify the compactly supported density. -> Weak spectral convergence without an atom at the origin.
- **lem-small-cocycle** [claim](#stmt-lem-small-cocycle) / [proof](#proof-stmt-lem-cocycle-spectrum-2): Choose a cutoff, invert above it, preserve real coefficients, and approximate by finite support. -> Small real finite coefficients with the prescribed approximate word value.
- **prop-perturbation** [claim](#stmt-prop-perturbation) / [proof](#proof-stmt-prop-perturbation): Compose the time-one flow with the inverse free-group basis transformation in the stated order. -> Approximation of the old completion by the moved word.
- **thm-rank-step** [claim](#stmt-thm-rank-step) / [proof](#proof-stmt-prop-perturbation-2): Choose approximating polynomials before the tolerance and future budgets after the witness word; prove generation separately by conditional expectation. -> A generating norm-limit Haar tuple and the rank isomorphism.
- **thm-main** [claim](#stmt-thm-main) / [proof](#proof-stmt-auto-remark-1): Compose consecutive-rank isomorphisms and amplify using the stated interpolation formula. -> The headline normal tracial isomorphism.
- **cor-all-interpolated** [claim](#stmt-cor-all-interpolated) / [proof](#proof-stmt-cor-all-interpolated): Exclude the pairwise-nonisomorphic alternative and use functorial amplification. -> All interpolated isomorphisms and their fundamental groups.
- **cor-entropy-dimensions** [claim](#stmt-cor-entropy-dimensions) / [proof](#proof-stmt-cor-entropy-dimensions): Transport semicircular tuples and algebraic group-algebra tuples while preserving their joint distributions. -> Different dimensions on generating tuples of the same factor.

<a id="pm-s4"></a>
## 4. Engineering Triptych

### Panel 1 - epistemic contract

- **I-CATALAN**: The noncrossing perfect matching count satisfies the first-partner recurrence and the Catalan formula.
- **I-GENERATION**: Haar word distribution and generation of the ambient algebra are separate requirements.
- **I-BUDGET**: Future error budgets are chosen after the witness word so that its Lipschitz cost is accommodated.

### Panel 2 - negative knowledge

- **A-FINITE**: Treat finite matching counts as a certificate of the factor isomorphism. -> The count does not establish spectral convergence, a prescribed cocycle vector, or generation by the limiting tuple.
- **A-DISTRIBUTION**: Infer generation from the limiting Haar word distribution alone. -> The limiting tuple may have the required distribution without a demonstrated dense word algebra.
- **A-BUDGET**: Fix future perturbation budgets before obtaining the witness word. -> A longer substituted word can increase the Lipschitz cost and invalidate the target approximation.
- **A-CUTOFF**: Replace the cutoff construction by an unchecked inverse or a uniform support bound. -> Small spectrum and the finite approximation error require the ordered choices in the proof.

### Panel 3 - reference implementation and oracle

Applicability: `constructive`. The noncrossing matching count is finite and constructive for each fixed order. The executed recurrence checks that combinatorial count; weak spectral convergence and the limiting generating tuple require separate analytic arguments.

```python
# INPUTS: An integer r in the admitted range 0 through 8, read from stdin.
# PRECONDITIONS: r is nonnegative and no greater than 8.
# OUTPUTS: The exact number of noncrossing perfect matchings on 2*r ordered positions.
# TIME: O(r*r) exact integer operations.
# SPACE: O(r) exact integers, excluding integer bit storage.
# ARITHMETIC: python-flint fmpz exact integers; no floating point.
# FAILURE: Invalid input exits nonzero; no approximate answer is returned.
import sys
from flint import fmpz
r = int(sys.stdin.read())
if not 0 <= r <= 8:
    raise ValueError("outside admitted range")
counts = [fmpz(1)]
for size in range(1, r + 1):
    total = fmpz(0)
    for split in range(size):
        total += counts[split] * counts[size - 1 - split]
    counts.append(total)
print(counts[r])
```

<a id="pm-s6"></a>
## 6. Applicability limits and search

- **L-rank** (hypothesis): The direct consecutive-rank construction assumes $n\geq3$; the rank-two conclusion additionally uses amplification. Blocked use: ['Applying the direct perturbation construction at rank two without the amplification argument.']
- **L-haar** (hypothesis): Identification requires both the Haar word distribution and generation of the ambient algebra. Blocked use: ['Inferring an onto isomorphism from the Haar word distribution alone.']
- **L-finite-support** (hypothesis): The polynomial flow uses finitely supported real coefficient vectors. Blocked use: ['Applying the stated polynomial-flow proof directly to arbitrary infinite-support or complex coefficient vectors.']
- **L-continuity** (edge_case): Point-norm continuity is established on the generated $C^*$-algebra, without a claim of point-norm continuity on every element of the ambient von Neumann algebra. Blocked use: ['Treating the normal extension as point-norm continuous on the entire ambient algebra.']
- **L-cutoff** (approximation): The cocycle construction uses a spectral cutoff and does not require an everywhere invertible positive operator. Blocked use: ['Replacing the cutoff inverse with an unrestricted inverse without checking its domain and boundedness.']
- **L-support-budget** (nonconstructive): The finite coefficient approximation is chosen after the spectral cutoff and prefix operator; a support-size bound uniform in accuracy is not supplied. Blocked use: ['Claiming a uniform finite support bound or a certified running-time bound for the full construction.']
- **L-adaptive** (approximation): Future error budgets are chosen after the witness word is known so that its word length is accommodated. Blocked use: ['Fixing future budgets independently of the substituted word polynomials while retaining the stated limiting approximation guarantee.']
- **L-completion** (edge_case): Only the first $n$ generators must converge in operator norm; the completions need not converge, and the target approximations are in $L^2$. Blocked use: ['Assuming convergence of the completion sequence or uniformly bounded operator norms of approximating polynomials.']
- **L-finite-certificate** (formal_scope): The factor isomorphism uses a norm limit and a separate density argument. Finite word and arithmetic checks certify only their finite subclaims. Blocked use: ['Presenting a finite word enumeration or moment calculation as a certificate of the full factor isomorphism.']
- **L-interpolation** (hypothesis): The extension to the infinite interpolated parameter invokes the classical dichotomy in addition to the constructed finite-rank isomorphism. Blocked use: ['Claiming the infinite-parameter conclusion from finite-rank iteration alone.']
- **L-entropy** (counterexample_scope): The entropy-dimension conclusion concerns changes of finite self-adjoint von Neumann generating tuples, without keeping their algebraic $*$-algebras fixed. Blocked use: ['Interpreting the corollary as a failure of algebraic-generator invariance for a fixed algebraic $*$-algebra.']

<a id="pm-formal"></a>
### Formal statement (Lean 4)

Corpus status: formalized in the source corpus (toolchain `leanprover/lean4:v4.34.1`, Mathlib `d13f23b723b8a846827a245b89c10fc7d3f11612`). Not re-checked here; the statements give the exact quantifiers and hypotheses, not a proof.

Scope note of family 287 (`lean/docs/287.md`, verbatim):

> The following describes the scope of the Lean formalization related to the following accompanying paper(s):
>
> - [An isomorphism of the free group factors](../../preprints/An-isomorphism-of-the-free-group-factors-September-23-2026/An-isomorphism-of-the-free-group-factors-September-23-2026.pdf)
>
> ## Scope
>
> The free group factor problem asks whether the von Neumann algebras of free groups of different ranks are isomorphic. The formalization proves that interpolated free group factors with any parameters $r,s>1$, including the infinite parameter, are normally trace-preservingly isomorphic. The paper's fundamental-group conclusion is a further consequence rather than a separate selected statement here.

```lean
import Mathlib

namespace OAI

noncomputable section

namespace FreeGroupFactorMain

universe u uG

open scoped ENNReal

abbrev GroupHilbert (G : Type u) := lp (fun _ : G => ℂ) 2
abbrev GroupOperator (G : Type u) := GroupHilbert G →L[ℂ] GroupHilbert G

def leftTranslations (G : Type u) [Group G] : Set (GroupOperator G) :=
  {T | ∃ g : G, ∀ (ξ : GroupHilbert G) (h : G), (T ξ) h = ξ (g⁻¹ * h)}

def groupVonNeumann (G : Type u) [Group G] : StarSubalgebra ℂ (GroupOperator G) :=
  StarSubalgebra.centralizer ℂ
    (StarSubalgebra.centralizer ℂ (leftTranslations G) : Set (GroupOperator G))

def canonicalTrace {G : Type u} [Group G] (T : groupVonNeumann G) : ℂ := by
  classical
  exact ((T : GroupOperator G) (lp.single 2 (1 : G) (1 : ℂ))) 1

@[instance_reducible] def ultraweakOperatorTopology (G : Type u) : TopologicalSpace (GroupOperator G) :=
  ⨅ (ξ : lp (fun _ : ℕ => GroupHilbert G) 2)
    (η : lp (fun _ : ℕ => GroupHilbert G) 2),
    TopologicalSpace.induced
      (fun T : GroupOperator G => ∑' k : ℕ, inner ℂ (η k) (T (ξ k)))
      inferInstance

@[instance_reducible] def groupUltraweakTopology (G : Type u) [Group G] :
    TopologicalSpace (groupVonNeumann G) :=
  TopologicalSpace.induced (fun T : groupVonNeumann G => (T : GroupOperator G))
    (ultraweakOperatorTopology G)

abbrev FreeGroupRank (n : ℕ) := FreeGroup (Fin n)

instance groupVN_isClosed (G : Type uG) [Group G] :
    IsClosed (groupVonNeumann G : Set (GroupOperator G)) := by
  unfold groupVonNeumann
  rw [StarSubalgebra.coe_centralizer]
  exact Set.isClosed_centralizer _

instance groupVN_CStarAlgebra (G : Type uG) [Group G] :
    CStarAlgebra (groupVonNeumann G) := inferInstance

end FreeGroupFactorMain

universe u_1 u_2 u_3 u_4 u_5 u_6 u_7 u_8 u_9 u_10 u_11 u_42 u_43

namespace FreeGroupFactorMain.Amplification

open scoped ENNReal

section
variable {A : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [CStarAlgebra A] [CStarAlgebra B]

def cornerSubalgebra (p : A) (hp : IsStarProjection p) : NonUnitalStarSubalgebra ℂ A where
  carrier := {x | p*x=x ∧ x*p=x}
  zero_mem' := by simp
  add_mem' hx hy := by constructor <;> simp [mul_add,add_mul,hx.1,hx.2,hy.1,hy.2]
  mul_mem' hx hy := by
    constructor
    · rw [← mul_assoc,hx.1]
    · rw [mul_assoc,hy.2]
  smul_mem' c x hx := by
    constructor
    · rw [mul_smul_comm,hx.1]
    · rw [smul_mul_assoc,hx.2]
  star_mem' hx := by
    constructor
    · simpa only [star_mul,hp.isSelfAdjoint.star_eq] using congrArg star hx.2
    · simpa only [star_mul,hp.isSelfAdjoint.star_eq] using congrArg star hx.1

abbrev Corner (p : A) (hp : IsStarProjection p) := ↥(cornerSubalgebra p hp)

instance cornerClosed (p : A) (hp : IsStarProjection p) :
    IsClosed (cornerSubalgebra p hp : Set A) :=
  (isClosed_eq (continuous_const.mul continuous_id) continuous_id).inter
    (isClosed_eq (continuous_id.mul continuous_const) continuous_id)

instance cornerSemiring (p : A) (hp : IsStarProjection p) : Semiring (Corner p hp) where
  __ : NonUnitalSemiring (Corner p hp) := inferInstance
  one := ⟨p,hp.isIdempotentElem.eq,hp.isIdempotentElem.eq⟩
  one_mul x := Subtype.ext x.property.1
  mul_one x := Subtype.ext x.property.2

instance cornerRing (p : A) (hp : IsStarProjection p) : Ring (Corner p hp) where
  __ : Semiring (Corner p hp) := inferInstance
  __ : NonUnitalRing (Corner p hp) := inferInstance

instance cornerAlgebra (p : A) (hp : IsStarProjection p) : Algebra ℂ (Corner p hp) :=
  Algebra.ofModule smul_mul_assoc mul_smul_comm

instance cornerNormedRing (p : A) (hp : IsStarProjection p) : NormedRing (Corner p hp) where
  __ : Ring (Corner p hp) := inferInstance
  __ : NonUnitalNormedRing (Corner p hp) := inferInstance

instance cornerCStarAlgebra (p : A) (hp : IsStarProjection p) : CStarAlgebra (Corner p hp) where
  __ : NonUnitalCStarAlgebra (Corner p hp) := inferInstance
  __ : NormedRing (Corner p hp) := inferInstance
  __ : Algebra ℂ (Corner p hp) := inferInstance
  norm_smul_le := norm_smul_le

end

section
variable {A : Type u_3} {B : Type u_4} [CStarAlgebra A] [CStarAlgebra B]

@[instance_reducible] def cornerTopology (𝒯 : TopologicalSpace A)
    (p : A) (hp : IsStarProjection p) : TopologicalSpace (Corner p hp) :=
  TopologicalSpace.induced (fun x : Corner p hp => (x : A)) 𝒯

def normalizedCornerTrace (τ : A → ℂ) (t : ℝ) (p : A) (hp : IsStarProjection p)
    (x : Corner p hp) : ℂ := (t : ℂ)⁻¹ * τ x

end

def stabilizedRightTranslations (G : Type u_5) [Group G] : Set (GroupOperator (ℕ × G)) :=
  {T | ∃ g : G, ∀ (ξ : GroupHilbert (ℕ × G)) (x : ℕ × G),
    T ξ x = ξ (x.1,x.2*g)}

def stabilization (G : Type u_6) [Group G] : StarSubalgebra ℂ (GroupOperator (ℕ × G)) :=
  StarSubalgebra.centralizer ℂ (stabilizedRightTranslations G)

instance stabilization_isClosed (G : Type u_7) [Group G] :
    IsClosed (stabilization G : Set (GroupOperator (ℕ × G))) := by
  rw [stabilization,StarSubalgebra.coe_centralizer]
  exact Set.isClosed_centralizer _

instance stabilization_CStarAlgebra (G : Type u_8) [Group G] : CStarAlgebra (stabilization G) :=
  inferInstance

def stabilizedProjectionTrace {G : Type u_9} [Group G] (p : stabilization G) : ℝ≥0∞ := by
  classical
  exact ∑' k : ℕ, ENNReal.ofReal (‖(p : GroupOperator (ℕ × G))
    (lp.single 2 (k,1) (1 : ℂ))‖ ^ 2)

def stabilizedTrace {G : Type u_10} [Group G] (T : stabilization G) : ℂ := by
  classical
  exact ∑' k : ℕ, inner ℂ (lp.single 2 (k,1) (1 : ℂ))
    ((T : GroupOperator (ℕ × G)) (lp.single 2 (k,1) (1 : ℂ)))

@[instance_reducible] def stabilizationTopology (G : Type u_11) [Group G] :
    TopologicalSpace (stabilization G) :=
  TopologicalSpace.induced (fun T : stabilization G => (T : GroupOperator (ℕ × G)))
    (ultraweakOperatorTopology (ℕ × G))

section
variable {A : Type u_42} {B : Type u_43} [CStarAlgebra A] [CStarAlgebra B]

structure NormalTracialEquiv (τA : A → ℂ) (τB : B → ℂ)
    (𝒯A : TopologicalSpace A) (𝒯B : TopologicalSpace B) where
  equiv : A ≃⋆ₐ[ℂ] B
  trace_eq : ∀x,τB (equiv x)=τA x
  normal : @Continuous _ _ 𝒯A 𝒯B equiv
  inverse_normal : @Continuous _ _ 𝒯B 𝒯A equiv.symm

end

end FreeGroupFactorMain.Amplification

namespace FreeGroupFactorMain.Interpolation

open Amplification
open scoped ENNReal

abbrev RankTwoProjection := {p : stabilization (FreeGroupRank 2) // IsStarProjection p}

instance rankTwoProjectionInhabited : Inhabited RankTwoProjection :=
  ⟨⟨0,IsStarProjection.zero _⟩⟩

def selectedProjection (a : ℝ) : RankTwoProjection :=
  Classical.epsilon (fun p : RankTwoProjection => stabilizedProjectionTrace p.val=ENNReal.ofReal a)

def interpolationScale (r : ℝ) : ℝ := (Real.sqrt (r-1))⁻¹

inductive Model where
  | integer (n : ℕ)
  | continuous (r : ℝ)
  | infinity

def Model.Algebra : Model → Type
  | .integer n => groupVonNeumann (FreeGroupRank n)
  | .continuous r => Corner (selectedProjection (interpolationScale r)).val
      (selectedProjection (interpolationScale r)).property
  | .infinity => groupVonNeumann (FreeGroup ℕ)

instance modelCStarAlgebra (m : Model) : CStarAlgebra m.Algebra := by
  cases m <;> dsimp only [Model.Algebra] <;> infer_instance

def Model.trace : (m : Model) → m.Algebra → ℂ
  | .integer _ => canonicalTrace
  | .continuous r => normalizedCornerTrace stabilizedTrace (interpolationScale r)
      (selectedProjection (interpolationScale r)).val (selectedProjection (interpolationScale r)).property
  | .infinity => canonicalTrace

@[instance_reducible] def Model.topology : (m : Model) → TopologicalSpace m.Algebra
  | .integer n => groupUltraweakTopology (FreeGroupRank n)
  | .continuous r => cornerTopology (stabilizationTopology (FreeGroupRank 2))
      (selectedProjection (interpolationScale r)).val (selectedProjection (interpolationScale r)).property
  | .infinity => groupUltraweakTopology (FreeGroup ℕ)

def parameterModel (r : ℝ≥0∞) : Model := by
  classical
  exact if r=⊤ then .infinity else
    if h : ∃n : ℕ,r=(n:ℝ≥0∞) then .integer h.choose else .continuous r.toReal

abbrev InterpolatedFactor (r : ℝ≥0∞) := (parameterModel r).Algebra
abbrev interpolatedTrace (r : ℝ≥0∞) := (parameterModel r).trace
abbrev interpolatedTopology (r : ℝ≥0∞) := (parameterModel r).topology

theorem allInterpolatedIsomorphic (r s : ℝ≥0∞) (hr : 1<r) (hs : 1<s) :
    Nonempty (NormalTracialEquiv (interpolatedTrace r) (interpolatedTrace s)
      (interpolatedTopology r) (interpolatedTopology s)) := by
  sorry

end FreeGroupFactorMain.Interpolation

end

end OAI
```

<a id="pm-s7"></a>
## 7. Complete mathematical paper


We solve the free group factor isomorphism problem affirmatively by proving that $`L(\mathbb F_2)`$ and $`L(\mathbb F_3)`$ are isomorphic as tracial von Neumann algebras. The classical free group factor alternative then implies that all interpolated free group factors, including $`L(\mathbb F_\infty)`$, are isomorphic and have fundamental group $`\mathbb R_{\gt 0}`$.

| [stmt-lem-flow-trace-identity](#stmt-lem-flow-trace-identity) | lemma | `lem:flow-trace-identity` | 9 | For every $p\in\mathcal P$ and every integer $r\geq1$, $$\tau\bigl(\operatorname{ev}_0(\delta^r p)\bigr)=0. |
| [stmt-lem-flow-global-ode](#stmt-lem-flow-global-ode) | lemma | `lem:flow-global-ode` | 10 | Let $E$ be any closed unital $C^*$-subalgebra of $M$ containing $S$, and let $V_1,\ldots,V_n$ be unitaries in $E$. |
| [stmt-lem-flow-moments](#stmt-lem-flow-moments) | lemma | `lem:flow-moments` | 11 | For every $p\in\mathcal P$ and $t\in\mathbb R$, |
| [stmt-lem-small-cocycle](#stmt-lem-small-cocycle) | lemma | `lem:small-cocycle` | 12 | Let $n\geq 3$ and $\eta>0$. |
| [stmt-lem-cocycle-prefixes](#stmt-lem-cocycle-prefixes) | lemma | `lem:cocycle-prefixes` | 13 | For each $m\geq 1$, the elements $p_1,\ldots,p_m$ freely generate a free subgroup of $\Gamma$. |
